and D must stay at chance in both roles)\n\nOf 30 members, the matched random axis D exceeds the empirical random-null reading band on 1 and induces refusal at >= 0.10 on 7.\n\n**The induction floor is a result, not a defect.** A random direction injected at axis A's OWN matched magnitude induces refusal at a rate of at least 0.10 on 7 of 30 members (max over the contrast grid; median across the panel 0.028, worst 0.389). This is a FLOOR that any steering claim has to clear, and it is measured here rather than assumed: the same magnitude that makes the canonical axis work also makes an arbitrary direction work on a substantial minority of models.\n\nOn the reading side, the empirical random-direction AUROC band spans +/-0.075 to +/-0.500 across members, so the textbook expectation that a random direction reads at 0.500 is wrong by a wide and model-dependent margin. An axis only demonstrates a directional finding if it \"\n                    \"sits outside this spread.\"}\n\n\ndef verdict_from_ci(lo: float, hi: float) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Pre-registered P4: AT_CHANCE / READS / AMBIGUOUS.\"\"\"\n    if not (np.isfinite(lo) and np.isfinite(hi)):\n        return \"UNDEFINED\"\n    if CHANCE_BAND[0] <= lo and hi <= CHANCE_BAND[1]:\n        return \"AT_CHANCE\"\n    if lo > READS_THRESHOLD:\n        return \"READS\"\n    return \"AMBIGUOUS\"\n\n\n# ==========================================================================\n# Detection statistics: stratum-centred, prompt-clustered\n# ==========================================================================\ndef centre_by_stratum(s: np.ndarray, strata: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Archived convention (analysis12._centre_by_stratum), verbatim semantics.\"\"\"\n    out = np.asarray(s, float).copy()\n    for st in np.unique(strata):\n        m = strata == st\n        if m.sum() >= 2:\n            out[m] -= out[m].mean()\n        else:\n            out[m] -= out.mean()\n    return out\n\n\ndef detection_stats(proj: dict, labels: np.ndarray, strata: np.ndarray,\n                    clusters: np.ndarray, n_boot: int = N_BOOT,\n                    seed: int = BOOT_SEED, centred: dict | None = None) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"AUROC per axis with a prompt-clustered bootstrap and a paired A-B contrast.\n\n    ``proj``    : {axis: (n,) projections onto the unit axis}\n    ``labels``  : bool, True = refusal\n    ``strata``  : per-item stratum name (harmless / harmful / xstest)\n    ``clusters``: per-item prompt uid (the bootstrap resampling unit)\n\n    Stratum-centring subtracts the per-stratum mean projection before pooling, so\n    a prompt-topic offset cannot inflate AUROC.  Raw (uncentred) AUROC is\n    reported alongside, never as the primary.\n    \"\"\"\n    labels = np.asarray(labels, bool)\n    axes = sorted(proj)\n    if centred is None:\n        centred = {ax: centre_by_stratum(proj[ax], strata) for ax in axes}\n    else:\n        centred = {ax: np.asarray(centred[ax], float) for ax in axes}\n\n    out = {\"n_items\": int(labels.size), \"n_refusal\": int(labels.sum()),\n           \"n_compliance\": int((~labels).sum()),\n           \"n_prompts\": int(len(np.unique(clusters))), \"axes\": {}}\n\n    # Stratum composition: if refusals live almost entirely in one stratum and\n    # compliances in another, a pooled AUROC can be driven by prompt topic rather\n    # than by refusal.  Centring removes the mean offset but not that separation,\n    # so a WITHIN-stratum AUROC is computed as well and the imbalance is reported.\n    comp, usable = {}, []\n    for s in np.unique(strata):\n        m = strata == s\n        nr, nc = int((labels & m).sum()), int(((~labels) & m).sum())\n        comp[str(s)] = {\"n_refusal\": nr, \"n_compliance\": nc, \"n\": int(m.sum())}\n        if nr >= 5 and nc >= 5:\n            usable.append(s)\n    out[\"stratum_composition\"] = comp\n    out[\"strata_with_both_classes\"] = [str(s) for s in usable]\n    nr_tot, nc_tot = int(labels.sum()), int((~labels).sum())\n    out[\"class_stratum_separation\"] = float(max(\n        max(v[\"n_refusal\"] / max(nr_tot, 1) for v in comp.values()),\n        max(v[\"n_compliance\"] / max(nc_tot, 1) for v in comp.values())))\n\n    boot_idx = list(cluster_boot_indices(clusters, n_boot, seed))\n    boot_auc: dict[str, list] = {ax: [] for ax in axes}\n    for idx in boot_idx:\n        yb = labels[idx]\n        if yb.sum() < 5 or (~yb).sum() < 5:", "numLines": 90, "startLine": 470, "totalLines": 716}}

Metric 1 therefore replaces the pooled figure with the only version that isolates the question actually asked -- across MODELS, within a SINGLE axis -- and metrics 4 and 5 do what a concession cannot: they quantify how much of 0.629 the control contrast supplies and attribute the pooled variance to its sources, which is the difference between admitting a confound and measuring it. Metric 10 is required because a paper whose thesis is measurement discipline cannot describe a gate its code does not implement; logging it as a deviation with its trigger is the standard this project already applies elsewhere.\n\nMetric 11 is the load-bearing repair. The claim 'abliteration removes the refusals, not the reader' is likely true, but as shipped it rests on four underpowered AUROCs plus one powered member -- five numbers a reviewer can dismiss in a sentence. Spontaneous refusal rates carry it independently and far more robustly: a median of 0.0076 in the weight-edited arm against 0.1131 in the aligned reference, over roughly 1,585 generations per member, with Wilson intervals and a within-lineage paired test over 10 abliterated-versus-parent pairs. Iteration 4's decisive diagnostic -- the archived 19-member block reproducing to 2.6e-4 while the 33 new members reversed -- was only interpretable because byte-exactness was proven first, and the iteration-4 aggregation repair failed to reproduce its own plan's -0.465 estimate. Reproducing 0.629, 0.448, 0.715 and all 30 per-member AUROCs to 1e-6 before restating anything is what separates a correction from a second, quieter error; and requiring the reviewer's 0.434/p = 0.14 to be reproduced (with the 13-member rule identified rather than glossed) makes the concession verifiable rather than merely gracious. The prose-pointer assertion extends the artifact's byte-identical-regeneration discipline to the sentences a reader actually reads, which is where iteration 4's 0.68/0.685/0.691 and 18-vs-20 discrepancies came from."

A statistic censored on half its rows is reported with that fact adjacent, never alone.\\n(c) The same within-axis analysis run SEPARATELY inside axis B and inside axis E, so the reader can see whether ANY single axis carries a within-axis coupling, plus axes C and D for completeness (expected null by construction -- if C or D shows a coupling comparable to A's, that is itself a finding about the pipeline and must be flagged loudly).\\n(d) TRIVIAL CONTROL LADDER on the pooled statistic: rho over all 70 pairs (= 0.629), then dropping D only, dropping C only, dropping both C and D, and finally A+B+E only. This is the quantity that shows exactly how much of 0.629 is the control contrast. Report each with its member/lineage-unit pair and pair count.\\n(e) NAME THE CONFOUND, do not merely concede it. Fit (i) a partial Spearman of detection on induction controlling for AXIS identity (rank-residualise both variables on axis dummies, then correlate residuals); (ii) the same controlling for MEMBER identity; (iii) a linear mixed-effects model on the ranked variables with an axis fixed effect and a member random intercept (statsmodels MixedLM, or a two-way ANOVA-style decomposition if convergence fails -- log which was used); and (iv) a VARIANCE DECOMPOSITION of the pooled statistic attributing it to between-axis-type, between-member and residual components, with each component's share summing to 1.0. Report the residual member-level coupling with its lineage-clustered CI.\\n(f) The within-member mean of 14 five-point coefficients recomputed (target 0.715) and labelled in the emitted prose as the mean of coefficients computed over the SAME axis-type contrast -- i.e. explicitly NOT independent evidence, and worse than the pooled figure rather than better.\\nVERDICT STRING, pre-committed with numeric triggers: COUPLING_SURVIVES_WITHIN_AXIS if the within-axis-A member-level rho >= 0.50 with a lineage-clustered CI excluding 0 AND the same sign at the lineage unit; COUPLING_IS_AXIS_TYPE_CONTRAST if the within-axis-A CI covers 0 while the pooled 70-pair CI excludes it and the variance decomposition attributes the majority share to between-axis-type; UNDERPOWERED if the CI covers 0 and its half-width exceeds 0.35 (at 7 lineages this is a live outcome and must be distinguishable from a null). Emit the deciding numbers alongside the string.\\n\\n=== STEP 3. ANALYSIS 2 -- H-K, THE VERDICT RULE ===\\n(a) DOUBLE TALLY of the axis-A verdicts: (i) as-is over all 30 members; (ii) restricted to detection-powered members (>= 40 per class, MIN_PER_CLASS from explib), which is the population the pre-registration says the statistic exists on. Both tallies cross-tabulated by the four arms of T1b (aligned_reference / weight_edited_abliteration / behavioural_uncensored_candidate / behavioural_uncensored_unverified), emitted as a ready-to-paste markdown table with row and column totals that are asserted to sum correctly.\\n(b) ATTAINABILITY SIMULATION -- this converts 'the rule is n-asymmetric' from a reviewer assertion into a measured operating characteristic. Simulate the SAME prompt-clustered percentile bootstrap the artifact applies (import cluster_boot_indices and detection_stats; reuse N_BOOT=2000 and the same >=5-per-class resample guard at explib.py:559, since that guard is what actually produces UNDEFINED). Grid: n per class in {5, 10, 20, 40, 80, 160}; true AUROC in {0.50, 0.55, 0.60, 0.69, 0.75, 0.90, 1.00} generated by two normal populations with the separation that yields that AUROC in closed form (d = sqrt(2)*Phi^-1(AUROC)); cluster structure in {1 item per prompt, 2 items per prompt, 4 items per prompt} because prompt clustering inflates CI width and the artifact's items ARE clustered; >= 2,000 simulation replicates per cell (parallelise over cells with multiprocessing -- 4 vCPUs, this is the only compute-heavy step and it is still minutes, not hours). Report per cell: P(READS), P(AT_CHANCE), P(AMBIGUOUS), P(UNDEFINED), mean CI width. From that surface extract the three answers the paper needs: (i) the MINIMUM n per class at which AT_CHANCE is attainable at all (whole 95% CI inside the 0.20-wide [0.40, 0.60] band) -- note in advance that the closed-form Hanley-McNeil variance implies this needs roughly 60-70 per class in the iid case and more under clustering, so the pre-registered >= 40 gate may itself be insufficient; verify, do not assume; (ii) P(READS | true AUROC = 0.50) as a function of n -- the false-positive rate of the READS rule; (iii) P(READS) under PERFECT separation at exactly n = 7, 12, 28, 32, 33, the counts at which the shipped T2 issues READS on unpowered members (with a discrete AUROC of 1.000 every bootstrap resample returns 1.000, so this is expected to be 1.0 at every n -- report the measured value). Deliver the one-sentence FOOTNOTE that must attach to every 'zero AT_CHANCE' sentence in the paper, generated from these numbers.\\n(c) GATE DEVIATION RECORD, with the exact code path. Quote both snippets with file:line, enumerate the affected members (from T2: BADMISTRAL_1p5B at 1/1, Qwen2p5_1p5B_Instruct_abliterated at 1/1, and the 0-refusal members Helvete_nano, Huihui_Qwen3_0p6B_abliterated_v2, Huihui_Qwen3_1p7B_abliterated_v2, Mia_001, Promt_generator, Qwen3_0p6B_abliterated, UNfilteredAI_1B), name the members that are UNPOWERED yet receive READS (DAN_Qwen3_1p7B 6/6, Josiefied_Qwen2p5_3B 12/12, Josiefied_Qwen3_4B 32/32, Llama_3p2_1B 28/28, Llama_3p2_1B_Instruct_abliterated 28/28, Qwen2p5_0p5B_Instruct_abliterated 33/33, TinyLlama 7/7 -- verify each count against the JSON rather than trusting this list), and log it in the project's standard deviation format {id, trigger, what_the_method_said, what_the_code_does, code_path, affected_members, correction_text}.\\n(d) THE ABLITERATED ARM, RESTATED ON ITS ACTUAL EVIDENCE. Ready-to-paste table for the 9 weight_edited_abliteration members giving per member: n refusals / n compliances, spontaneous refusal rate, powered flag, axis-A AUROC with CI, verdict. Make explicit that the arm's 5 READS rest on ONE powered member plus four underpowered ones. Then verify the STRUCTURAL claim INDEPENDENTLY OF ANY AUROC: compute Wilson 95% intervals on each member's spontaneous refusal rate (numerator = refusals, denominator = the member's generation count, ~1,585 per member -- read the actual denominator per member, do not assume), report the arm medians (0.0076 weight-edited, 0.0000 behavioural-uncensored candidate, 0.1131 aligned reference), and test the weight-edited arm against the aligned-reference arm with (i) a member-level exact Wilcoxon rank-sum / Mann-Whitney U on the rates, (ii) a lineage-clustered bootstrap of the difference in medians, and (iii) the WITHIN-LINEAGE paired comparison already tabulated in T2b (10 abliterated-vs-parent pairs; exact paired sign test plus the paired rate differences). If and only if these carry the claim, the emitted prose states 'abliteration removes the refusals, not the reader' on refusal-RATE evidence and cites the four underpowered AUROCs as illustration only. If they do not carry it, the prose says the structural claim is not established and reports what would be needed.\\n\\n=== STEP 4. DELIVERABLES ===\\neval_out.json (schema-validated with the aii-json skill; also emit mini_ and preview_ variants and run the aii-file-size-limit check) containing: provenance (every input path + sha256 + size, the prereg hash, the missing-file log, library versions, wall-clock, and the explicit statement '$0.00 LLM spend, zero GPU, zero generation'); reproduction_gate (every leg with target, obtained, delta, tolerance, PASS/FAIL); analysis1 (every coefficient from 2a-2f at BOTH units with CI, n_units, permutation p and the 1/5040 floor flag; the 0.434/p=0.14 reproduction leg with the identified 13-member rule; the variance decomposition shares; the verdict string with its deciding numbers); analysis2 (both tallies cross-tabulated by arm, the full simulation surface as arrays plus the three extracted answers, the deviation record, the abliterated-arm table and the three refusal-rate tests); and replacement_text.\\nREPLACEMENT-TEXT BUNDLE, GENERATED FROM THE JSON, never hand-typed (the stage4_prose pattern from the iteration-4 evaluation): (i) the drop-in H-C paragraph demoting the 70-pair rho to an explicitly labelled secondary and leading with the within-axis estimate; (ii) the corrected Method sentence for the UNDEFINED gate; (iii) the attainability footnote; (iv) the double-tally table; (v) the abliterated-arm table and its restated claim; (vi) the deviation record entry. Every number in the prose carries a JSON pointer (e.g. {analysis1.primary.member.rho|0.434}) and the script ENDS with an executed assertion that walks the emitted prose, resolves every pointer against eval_out.json, and fails the run if any number in the text is untraceable or mismatched -- the same discipline RESULTS.md already applies to itself, extended to the prose.\\nAlso emit RESULTS.md rendered from eval_out.json so it regenerates byte-identically, and 2-3 vector figures via the aii-data-fig-gen skill: the within-axis-A scatter with the pooled 70-pair scatter beside it (the visual statement of the confound), the control ladder as a forest plot of rho by axis subset at both units, and the attainability surface as a heatmap of P(AT_CHANCE) over n x true-AUROC.\\n\\n=== STEP 5. FAILURE MODES AND WHAT TO DO ===\\n(1) Reproduction gate G1 fails -> STOP the restatement, spend the remaining time diagnosing (likely candidates: which 14 members entered T6, whether max-rate or c_50 was the x variable, the tie-handling in Spearman, the bootstrap seed), and ship a diagnostic-only eval_out.json with the gate table and the diagnosis. A failed gate honestly reported is worth more than a restatement on unreproduced numbers.\\n(2) proj_*_items.json missing for a member -> drop to summary-level reproduction for that member, label the leg, never impute.\\n(3) MixedLM fails to converge on 70 points -> fall back to the rank-residualised partial correlation plus the two-way variance decomposition, and log the fallback with the convergence error.\\n(4) The within-axis-A rho comes out HIGH and CI-excluding-zero -> that is COUPLING_SURVIVES_WITHIN_AXIS; still ship the full control ladder and decomposition, and still state that the pooled 0.629 was confounded.\\n(5) The rho comes out near 0.43 with a CI covering zero -> emit the reviewer's honest sentence verbatim as pre-registered. Salvage phrasing ('trending', 'marginally significant', 'suggestive') is banned by the prereg and the assertion pass should grep for those tokens and fail on them.\\n(6) The simulation shows AT_CHANCE IS attainable at n around 40 -> then the rule is less asymmetric than claimed; report that, and narrow the H-K concession to the READS side (which the perfect-separation cells will still indict).\\n(7) Time pressure (3 h budget): the ordering is Step 1 gate -> Step 3a/3c/3d (cheap, table-shaped, and they alone satisfy most of H-K) -> Step 2a/2d (the primary and the control ladder) -> Step 3b simulation -> Step 2e -> figures. Ship whatever completes with an explicit completed/not-completed manifest at the top of eval_out.json; a partial run with a truthful manifest is reportable, a silently truncated one is not.\",\n  \"metrics_descriptions\": \"H-C METRICS (all at BOTH aggregation units -- member and lineage -- with lineage-clustered percentile bootstrap CIs at 10,000 reps, the number of resampling units printed beside every CI, and an exhaustive 5040-permutation p with the 1/5040 = 1.98e-4 attainable floor flagged):\\n1. PRIMARY: within-axis-A across-member Spearman rho between induction quality (axis-A maximum induced refusal rate, T3) and detection quality (axis-A held-out AUROC on the model's own spontaneous text, T2), over the detection-powered members (14 on disk; the reviewer's 13-member recompute of 0.434 / p = 0.14 is reproduced as a required gate leg, with the identifying exclusion rule named).\\n2. SECONDARY within-axis-A with x = -log10(axis-A c_50), reported with its censoring fraction and the rank_bottom sentinel convention for the 7 censored members.\\n3. Within-axis rho computed separately inside axes B and E (and C, D for completeness), answering whether ANY single axis carries a within-axis coupling.\\n4. CONTROL LADDER on the pooled statistic: rho over all 70 (member, axis) pairs, then minus D, minus C, minus C and D, then A+B+E only -- quantifying exactly how much of 0.629 is the by-construction control contrast.\\n5. CONFOUND-NAMING estimates: rank-residualised partial Spearman controlling for axis identity; the same controlling for member identity; a mixed-effects fit on ranks with an axis fixed effect and member random intercept; and a variance decomposition of the pooled statistic into between-axis-type / between-member / residual shares summing to 1.0.\\n6. The within-member mean of 14 five-point coefficients (target 0.715), reported as the mean of coefficients over the same axis-type contrast and therefore NOT independent evidence.\\n7. Verdict string COUPLING_SURVIVES_WITHIN_AXIS (within-axis-A member rho >= 0.50, CI excluding 0, same sign at the lineage unit) / COUPLING_IS_AXIS_TYPE_CONTRAST (within-axis CI covers 0, pooled CI excludes it, majority variance share between-axis-type) / UNDERPOWERED (CI covers 0 and half-width > 0.35), emitted with its deciding numbers.\\n\\nH-K METRICS:\\n8. Axis-A verdict tally (READS / AMBIGUOUS / AT_CHANCE / UNDEFINED) reported TWICE -- as-is over 30 members, and restricted to detection-powered members (>= 40 per class) -- each cross-tabulated by the four arms, with totals asserted.\\n9. OPERATING CHARACTERISTIC of the verdict rule, from simulation of the same prompt-clustered percentile bootstrap over n per class {5,10,20,40,80,160} x true AUROC {0.50,0.55,0.60,0.69,0.75,0.90,1.00} x cluster size {1,2,4}, >= 2,000 replicates per cell: P(READS), P(AT_CHANCE), P(AMBIGUOUS), P(UNDEFINED) and mean CI width per cell. Three extracted quantities: the minimum n at which AT_CHANCE (whole CI inside [0.40,0.60]) is attainable at all; P(READS | true AUROC = 0.50) versus n; and P(READS) under perfect separation at n = 7, 12, 28, 32, 33.\\n10. GATE DEVIATION record: the exact code path (explib.verdict_from_ci returns UNDEFINED only on non-finite CI bounds, which arise from the >= 5-per-class bootstrap-resample guard; MIN_PER_CLASS = 40 governs only the separate `powered` flag), the affected members, and the corrected Method sentence.\\n11. ABLITERATED-ARM restatement: per-member refusal/compliance counts, spontaneous refusal rate with Wilson 95% interval, powered flag, AUROC with CI and verdict for all 9 weight-edited members; arm medians (0.0076 / 0.0000 / 0.1131); and three AUROC-free tests of the structural claim -- exact Mann-Whitney on member rates versus the aligned-reference arm, a lineage-clustered bootstrap of the median difference, and the exact paired sign test over the 10 within-lineage abliterated-vs-parent pairs of T2b.\\n\\nVALIDITY METRICS: a reproduction gate of 7 legs (pooled rho 0.629 and its CI, the c_50 secondary 0.448 with censoring 0.771, the within-member mean 0.715, all 30 per-member AUROCs and CIs re-bootstrapped from stored items at the archived seed, the T1b arm table, the verdict tally including resolution of the 18/0/10-vs-20/1/9 discrepancy, and the lineage-id-string-versus-lineage-count check), each with target, obtained, delta and tolerance 1e-6; plus an executed assertion that every number in the generated prose resolves to a JSON pointer in eval_out.json.\",\n  \"metrics_justification\": \"The hypothesis's surviving positive is that reading and steering along one refusal axis are NOT dissociated. Right now that positive is carried by rho = 0.629 over 70 (member, axis) pairs, and the reviewer has shown -- from this study's own shipped tables -- that the statistic is dominated by a between-axis-TYPE contrast: axis A is strong in both roles by construction and axes C and D are null in both roles by construction, so pooling them measures the difference between a fitted axis and a random one, not a relationship between induction and reading among models. A lineage bootstrap cannot repair this because the confound is within-member. Metric 1 therefore replaces the pooled figure with the only version that isolates the question actually asked -- across MODELS, within a SINGLE axis -- and metrics 4 and 5 do what a concession cannot: they quantify how much of 0.629 the control contrast supplies and attribute the pooled variance to its sources, which is the difference between admitting a confound and measuring it. Metric 3 checks whether the finding is axis-A-specific or a general property of fitted directions, which changes what the paper is allowed to claim. Metric 6 exists to demote a number that currently reads as corroboration (0.715 > 0.629) but is the mean of 14 coefficients over the same contrast, so it is weaker evidence, not stronger; reporting it without that label would repeat the original error.\\n\\nThe verdict-rule metrics matter because 'zero AT_CHANCE over 30 checkpoints' is the sentence doing the most work in the reversal, and it is partly a property of the rule rather than of the models: READS needs only a CI lower bound above 0.60, which perfect separation on a handful of items delivers automatically, while AT_CHANCE needs an entire bootstrap CI inside a 0.20-wide band, which AUROC sampling variance makes unreachable at n around 10. Metric 9 converts that from assertion to a measured operating characteristic on the artifact's own estimator, yielding the minimum n at which the null verdict is even attainable and the false-READS rate at true chance -- the footnote that must accompany every 'zero AT_CHANCE' claim, and the number that tells a reader whether the tally is evidence or arithmetic. Metric 8's double tally restricts the count to the population the pre-registration says the statistic exists on, which is the only tally a reviewer will accept. Metric 10 is required because a paper whose thesis is measurement discipline cannot describe a gate its code does not implement; logging it as a deviation with its trigger is the standard this project already applies elsewhere.\\n\\nMetric 11 is the load-bearing repair. The claim 'abliteration removes the refusals, not the reader' is likely true, but as shipped it rests on four underpowered AUROCs plus one powered member -- five numbers a reviewer can dismiss in a sentence. Spontaneous refusal rates carry it independently and far more robustly: a median of 0.0076 in the weight-edited arm against 0.1131 in the aligned reference, over roughly 1,585 generations per member, with Wilson intervals and a within-lineage paired test over 10 abliterated-versus-parent pairs. Moving the claim onto the rate evidence makes it survive the loss of the AUROCs, which is exactly what H-K asks for.\\n\\nFinally, the reproduction gate is not bureaucracy.
